Title: Histoire du fanatisme de notre tems; nouvelle édition augmentée du Traité de l'obéissance des chrétiens aux puissances temporelles; par Mr. de Brueys; tomes premier et second
Creator: David-Augustin de Brueys
Publisher: La Haye: de l'imprimerie de H. Scheurleer, F. Z.
Publication Date: 1755
Rights: Public Domain (published 1755, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'http://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/')
Language: fra
Source: Internet Archive item bub_gb_4zCYRO3E_0YC (https://archive.org/details/bub_gb_4zCYRO3E_0YC), the item's own bub_gb_4zCYRO3E_0YC_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: unknown library; host metadata date: 1755. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 643a53481816ab668548cf3c0b200a395e5223de6e234ccef1e7087759621495.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Google Books scan re-hosted on the Internet Archive (contributing library not named); tomes one and two bound in one item. Good OCR with the long s read as f; 'Digitized by Google' lines interleaved at page breaks are left in place.
Content note: The convert controversialist's history of 'fanaticism': book one on the prophets of Dauphiné and Vivarais in 1688-1689 (Isabeau Vincent and her successors, and Jurieu's part in them), then the Camisard war year by year; the persecuting side's account, written by a former Protestant at Montpellier.
